<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>囥弗住</rb><rt>kʽông-feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>芦竿</rb><rt>lu-ken</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>箱子</rb><rt>siang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石油</rb><rt>zah-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>乳浆</rb><rt>jü-tsiang</rt><rb>涂勒</rb><rt>du-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>安来</rb><rt>en-læ</rt><rb>河边沿</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-yin</rt><rb>芦柴缝里</rb><rt>lu-za-vong-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿姊</rb><rt>ah-tsi</rt><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>河边沿</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-yin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滰肉</rb><rt>gyiang-nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>河边沿</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-yin</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箱子</rb><rt>siang-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>芦柴缝里</rb><rt>lu-za-vong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>开开来</rb><rt>Kʽæ-kʽæ-læ</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>叫</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿姊</rb><rt>ah-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>阿哺</rb><rt>ah-bu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>养</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>奶花个</rb><rt>na-hwun-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>抱</rb><rt>Bao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>养</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>养</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>大起来</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>囡个</rb><rt>nön-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>水里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>拉</rb><rt>l̆a</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上来个</rb><rt>zông-læ-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Te-cʽih. 就是𠡒出。（原注）</sup>

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>壮年</rb><rt>công-nyin</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>挑担</rb><rt>tʽiao-tæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>Kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>囥</rb><rt>kʽông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>沙泥里</rb><rt>sô-nyi-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相打</rb><rt>siang-tang</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>弗成礼</rb><rt>feh-dzing-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>自家人</rb><rt>zi-kô-nying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>破了</rb><rt>pʽo-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>避过</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>坐来</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb><u>米甸</u>个</rb><rt>Mi-din-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼满</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>水槽</rb><rt>shü-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>羊队</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>看羊个</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起来</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>羊队</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>归到</rb><rt>kyü-tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流珥</rb><rt>Liu-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末子</rb><rt>Kyih-mih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>看羊</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>羊队</rb><rt>yiang-de</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>错落</rb><rt>tsʽo-loh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>吃饼</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ping</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西坡拉</rb><rt>Si-pʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革顺</rb><rt>Keh-jing</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Cʽih-meng nying. 就是出门人。（原注）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>劳苦</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叹气</rb><rt>tʽæn-kyʽi</rt><rb>讴响</rb><rt>eo-hyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>劳苦个</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>叹气个</rb><rt>tʽæn-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>设立个</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看顾</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照管</rb><rt>tsiao-kwun</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. III.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>叶罗</u>个</rb><rt>Yih-lo-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>外边</rb><rt>nga-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>何烈</rb><rt>ʽO-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>刺棚树</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang-jü</rt><rb>火焰</rb><rt>ho-yin</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>刺棚树</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang-jü</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>dziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>倒走转</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>刺棚里</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang-li</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>！」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>脱掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>脚里个</rb><rt>kyiah-li-go</rt><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>立的个</rb><rt>lih-tih-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>遮勒</rb><rt>tsô-leh</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>督工</rb><rt>toh-kong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怀怀</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>讴响</rb><rt>eo-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-loh-læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>宽阔</rb><rt>kwʽun-kwʽeh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>厚实</rb><rt>ʽeo-jih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>奶奶</rb><rt>na-na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖个</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份个</rb><rt>dzoh-veng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>磨难</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>？」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>自然而然</rb><rt>Z-JÜN-R-JÜN</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>YIU-GO</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>自然</rb><rt>Z-JÜN</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>YIU-GO</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>Tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>永远个</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代个</rb><rt>dæ-dæ-go</rt><rb>记号</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>。』<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真正</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>照管</rb><rt>tsiao-kwun</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>话过了</rb><rt>wô-ko-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份个</rb><rt>dzoh-veng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>奶奶</rb><rt>na-na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖个</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」』<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>会着</rb><rt>we-djoh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>三日个</rb><rt>sæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能干个</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手段</rb><rt>siu-dön</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>弗许</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走出个</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>空手</rb><rt>kʽong-siu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>个打个</rb><rt>Ko-tang-ko</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋里个</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>女客</rb><rt>nyü-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>银子个</rb><rt>nying-ts-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Z-jün-r-jün yiu-go. 就是自然而然有个。（原注）</sup>

### C. IV.

<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>MO-SI</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>手里个</rb><rt>siu-li-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>Kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>丢</rb><rt>Tiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-loh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>一梗</rb><rt>ih-kwang</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>尾巴</rb><rt>mi-pô</rt></ruby>。」（<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>。）<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>叉进</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>怀里</rb><rt>gwa-li</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>叉进</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>怀里</rb><rt>gwa-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻风</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雪</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>叉进</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>怀里</rb><rt>gwa-li</rt></ruby>。」（<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>叉进</rb><rt>tsʽô-tsing</rt><rb>怀里</rb><rt>gwa-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>余多个</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。）<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>倘然</rb><rt>Tʽông-jün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>河里个</rb><rt>ʽo-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒来</rb><rt>tao-læ</rt><rb>燥地里</rb><rt>sao-di-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>河里</rb><rt>ʽo-li</rt><rb>驮来个</rb><rt>do-læ-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>燥地里</rb><rt>sao-di-li</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>嗄</rb><rt>ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>da</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>口能</rb><rt>kʽeo-neng</rt><rb>舌辩</rb><rt>zih-bin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>Zao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嘴巴个</rb><rt>cü-pô-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>Zao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>聋𦗭</rb><rt>long-bang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>亮眼</rb><rt>liang-ngæn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>瞎眼个</rb><rt>hah-ngæn-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>Gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>差个</rb><rt>tsʽa-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>托付</rb><rt>tʽoh-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>捏</rb><rt>nyiah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>根</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叶罗</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>转去</rb><rt>cün-kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>叶罗</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安安</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>稳稳</rb><rt>w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>性命个</rb><rt>sing-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>骑</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>放来</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>手下个</rb><rt>siu-ʽô-go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>长子</rb><rt>tsiang-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>Fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>长子</rb><rt>tsiang-ts</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>下处里</rb><rt>ʽô-cʽü-li</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西坡拉</rb><rt>Si-pʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>火石</rb><rt>ho-zah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>割</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>下身个</rb><rt>ʽô-sing-go</rt><rb>皮</rb><rt>bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出血个</rb><rt>cʽih-hyüih-go</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>西坡拉</rb><rt>Si-pʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出血个</rb><rt>cʽih-hyüih-go</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>亲</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘴</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>传向</rb><rt>djün-hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>照管</rb><rt>tsiao-kwun</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看顾</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>讴带倒</rb><rt>eo-tæn-tao</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. V.

<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEO-DEO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>Fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>识得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>会着</rb><rt>we-djoh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野里</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>三日个</rb><rt>sæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>刀枪</rb><rt>tao-tsʽiang</rt><rb>攻打</rb><rt>kong-tang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挑</rb><rt>tʽiao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>担</rb><rt>tæn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>歇担</rb><rt>hyih-tæn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>当日</rb><rt>Tông-nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>督工个</rb><rt>toh-kong-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>作头</rb><rt>tsoh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>麦秆</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>砖头</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>麦秆</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>砖头个</rb><rt>cün-deo-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做满</rb><rt>tso-mun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>减落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>懒惰</rb><rt>læn-do</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>劳苦</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>睬</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>空头</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>督工个</rb><rt>toh-kong-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>作头</rb><rt>tsoh-deo</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>麦秆</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。』<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着个</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>麦秆</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>减落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>拔</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>草</rb><rt>tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>根株</rb><rt>keng-cü</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>麦秆</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>督工个</rb><rt>Toh-kong-go</rt><rb>催</rb><rt>tsʽe</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>逐日</rb><rt>djoh-nyih</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>麦秆</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>督工个</rb><rt>toh-kong-go</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>作头</rb><rt>tsoh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>昨日</rb><rt>Zô-nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>砖头</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>照数</rb><rt>tsiao-su</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>作头</rb><rt>tsoh-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>？<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>麦秆</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>砖头</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔自个</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>过失</rb><rt>ko-shih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>懒惰</rb><rt>læn-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>懒惰</rb><rt>læn-do</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔<u>拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>麦</u>秆</rb><rt>mah-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照数</rb><rt>tsiao-su</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>砖头</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>作头</rb><rt>tsoh-deo</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>Me-nyih</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>砖头</rb><rt>cün-deo</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>减落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt></ruby>」，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>受害</rb><rt>ziu-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>Li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出个</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>路街里</rb><rt>lu-ka-li</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城子个</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>贼臭个</rb><rt>zah-tsʽiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>？<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djoh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>暴虐</rb><rt>bao-nyiah</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」
