### C. VI.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做拨</rb><rt>tso-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>能干个</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>能干个</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>上前</rb><rt>Zông-zin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>全能</rb><rt>Djün-neng</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗晓得</rb><rt>feh-hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>耽搁个</rb><rt>tæn-koh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>暴虐</rb><rt>bao-nyiah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>叹气</rb><rt>tʽæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>重担</rb><rt>djong-tæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>歇工</rb><rt>hyih-kong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>重重</rb><rt>djong-djong</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>拣</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>重担个</rb><rt>djong-tæn-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-koh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>悲伤</rb><rt>pe-sông</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>苦个</rb><rt>kwʽu-go</rt><rb>重担</rb><rt>djong-tæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听从</rb><rt>tʽing-dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>11</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>奏明</rb><rt>tseo-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>嘴唇</rb><rt>cü-jing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割</rb><rt>tsiu-keh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>众族</rb><rt>cong-dzoh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>头个</rb><rt>deo-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>哈诺</rb><rt>Ha-noh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>法路</rb><rt>Fah-lu</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希斯伦</rb><rt>Hyi-s-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>迦米</rb><rt>Kyüô-mi</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>流便</u>个</rb><rt>Liu-bin-go</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb><u>西缅</u>个</rb><rt>Si-min-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶母利</rb><rt>Yiæ-meo-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅悯</rb><rt>Yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>阿辖</rb><rt>O-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅斤</rb><rt>Yüô-kying</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>琐辖</rb><rt>So-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>少罗</rb><rt>Siao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西缅</u>个</rb><rt>Si-min-go</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>革顺</rb><rt>Keh-jing</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>哥辖</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>米拉利</rb><rt>Mi-lah-li</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>利未</u>个</rb><rt>Li-vi-go</rt><rb>寿岁</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十七</rb><rt>sæn-jih-tsʽih</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb><u>革顺</u>个</rb><rt>Keh-jing-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>立尼</rb><rt>Lih-nyi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>示每</rb><rt>Z-me</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb><u>哥辖</u>个</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>暗兰</rb><rt>En-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以斯哈</rb><rt>Yi-s-ha</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希伯仑</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>乌薛</rb><rt>U-sih</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>哥辖</u>个</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh-go</rt><rb>寿岁</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十三</rb><rt>sæn-jih-sæn</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb><u>米拉利</u>个</rb><rt>Mi-lah-li-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>抹利</rb><rt>Meh-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>母示</rb><rt>Meo-z</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>利未</u>个</rb><rt>Li-vi-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>暗兰</rb><rt>En-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿姑</rb><rt>ah-kwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约基别</rb><rt>Iah-kyi-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>暗兰</rb><rt>En-læn</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>暗兰</u>个</rb><rt>En-læn-go</rt><rb>寿岁</rb><rt>ziu-shü</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十七</rb><rt>sæn-jih-tsʽih</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以斯哈</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-s-ha-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>可拉</rb><rt>Kʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>尼弗</rb><rt>Nyi-feh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>色利</rb><rt>Seh-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb><u>乌薛</u>个</rb><rt>U-sih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>米沙利</rb><rt>Mi-sô-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利撒反</rb><rt>Yi-li-sah-fæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>色利</rb><rt>Seh-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚米拿达</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-mi-nô-dah-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb><u>拿顺</u>个</rb><rt>Nô-jing-go</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>以利沙巴</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿搭</rb><rt>Nô-tah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比户</rb><rt>Üô-pi-wu</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以大玛</rb><rt>Yi-da-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb><u>可拉</u>个</rb><rt>Kʽo-lah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚惜</rb><rt>Üô-sih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利加拿</rb><rt>Yi-li-kyüô-nô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比亚撒</rb><rt>Üô-pi-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>可拉</u>个</rb><rt>Kʽo-lah-go</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>部铁</rb><rt>Bu-tʽih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非尼哈</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-ha</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>头个</rb><rt>deo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>房份</rb><rt>vông-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>兵队</rb><rt>ping-de</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>嘴唇</rb><rt>cü-jing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>周割</rb><rt>tsiu-keh</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

---

### C. VII.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>重重</rb><rt>djong-djong</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兵队</rb><rt>ping-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是介</rb><rt>ziu-z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>八十</rb><rt>pah-jih</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>八十三</rb><rt>pah-jih-sæn</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>邪术</rb><rt>zia-jih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>博老</rb><rt>poh-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>法术</rb><rt>fah-jih</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>Koh-nying</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>吞吃</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>Ming-tsiao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>河沿</rb><rt>ʽo-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>河边沿</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-yin</rt><rb>等候</rb><rt>teng-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>蛇个</rb><rt>dzô-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>Fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野里</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏个</rb><rt>nyiah-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>河里个</rb><rt>ʽo-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>河里个</rb><rt>ʽO-li-go</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>贼臭</rb><rt>zah-tsʽiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>河里个</rb><rt>ʽo-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>吃弗来</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。」』」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>水个</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>江</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>池</rb><rt>dzi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>聚拢个</rb><rt>jü-long-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>变做</rb><rt>pin-tso</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>树木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>到处</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>驮去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>河里个</rb><rt>ʽo-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>臣子个</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>河里个</rb><rt>ʽo-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>河里个</rb><rt>ʽO-li-go</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>贼臭了</rb><rt>zah-tsʽiu-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>河里</rb><rt>ʽo-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃弗来</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>到处</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>博老</rb><rt>poh-lao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>法术</rb><rt>fah-jih</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>王宫</rb><rt>wông-kong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>河里</rb><rt>ʽo-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>吃弗来</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-feh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>河边沿</rb><rt>ʽo-pin-yin</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>掘落去</rb><rt>gyüih-loh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. VIII.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>Fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>tsʽing-w̆ô</rt><rb>吵扰</rb><rt>tsʽao-ziao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>团圈个</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>河里</rb><rt>ʽO-li</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>tsʽing-w̆ô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上来</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>困个</rb><rt>kwʽeng-go</rt><rb>房里</rb><rt>vông-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>眠床</rb><rt>min-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>风炉</rb><rt>fong-lu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>面粉个</rb><rt>min-feng-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>Tsʽing-w̆ô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。」』」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>江</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>池个</rb><rt>dzi-go</rt><rb>上顶</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>tsʽing-w̆ô</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>水个</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>上顶</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>tsʽing-w̆ô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>充满</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>博老</rb><rt>Poh-lao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>法术</rb><rt>fah-jih</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>tsʽing-w̆ô</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>tsʽing-w̆ô</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>放出</rb><rt>fông-cʽih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>tsʽing-w̆ô</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>河里</rb><rt>ʽo-li</rt><rb>剩落东</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>Ming-tsiao</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>Tsʽing-w̆ô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>河里</rb><rt>ʽo-li</rt><rb>剩落东</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>引</rb><rt>ying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>tsʽing-w̆ô</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>明堂</rb><rt>ming-dông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田里个</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>tsʽing-w̆ô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>收其拢</rb><rt>siu-gyi-long</rt><rb>堆打堆</rb><rt>te-tang-te</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>贼臭了</rb><rt>zah-tsʽiu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>宽弛了</rb><rt>kwʽun-s-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>听从</rb><rt>tʽing-dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>驮起</rb><rt>Do-kyʽi</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>地土个</rb><rt>di-tʽu-go</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>变做</rb><rt>pin-tso</rt><rb>虱</rb><rt>seh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就是介</rb><rt>ziu-z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>地土个</rb><rt>di-tʽu-go</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>变做</rb><rt>pin-tso</rt><rb>虱</rb><rt>seh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口个</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地土个</rb><rt>di-tʽu-go</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>变做</rb><rt>pin-tso</rt><rb>虱</rb><rt>seh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>博老</rb><rt>Poh-lao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>法术</rb><rt>fah-jih</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>变做</rb><rt>pin-tso</rt><rb>虱</rb><rt>seh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>变弗来</rb><rt>pin-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>虱</rb><rt>seh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口个</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>博老</rb><rt>Poh-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>手段</rb><rt>siu-dön</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>听从</rb><rt>tʽing-dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>立来</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>Fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>苍蝇</rb><rt>tsʽông-ing</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>苍蝇</rb><rt>tsʽông-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>充满</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>分别</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉间个</rb><rt>deng-kæn-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歌珊</rb><rt>Ko-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>苍蝇</rb><rt>tsʽông-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>分别</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>。」』」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>潮数</rb><rt>dziao-su</rt><rb>苍蝇</rb><rt>tsʽông-ing</rt><rb>飞进</rb><rt>fi-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>臣子个</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>苍蝇</rb><rt>tsʽông-ing</rt><rb>坏掉了</rb><rt>wæ-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>可恶个</rb><rt>kʽo-u-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>可恶个</rb><rt>kʽo-u-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>献祭</rb><rt>hyin-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野里</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt><rb>走勒</rb><rt>tseo-leh</rt><rb>三日个</rb><rt>sæn-nyih-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野里</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>走远</rb><rt>tseo-yün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>苍蝇</rb><rt>tsʽông-ing</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>哄骗</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>苍蝇</rb><rt>tsʽông-ing</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. IX.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>Fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>硬留</rb><rt>ngang-liu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>牲口个</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>马</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>骆驼</rb><rt>loh-do</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>重重个</rb><rt>djong-djong-go</rt><rb>瘟疫</rb><rt>w̆eng-yüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>分别</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」』」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>Ming-tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第二日</rb><rt>di-nyi-nyih</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>风炉里</rb><rt>fong-lu-li</rt><rb>灰</rb><rt>hwe</rt><rb>一把</rb><rt>ih-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>散开去</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>变做</rb><rt>pin-tso</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>团圈个</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口个</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>发出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>疮毒</rb><rt>tsʽông-doh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>风炉里</rb><rt>fong-lu-li</rt><rb>灰</rb><rt>hwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立来</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>散开去</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>疮毒</rb><rt>tsʽông-doh</rt><rb>发出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口个</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>博老</rb><rt>Poh-lao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>疮个</rb><rt>tsʽông-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗能够</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>立来</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>博老</rb><rt>poh-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>疮毒</rb><rt>tsʽông-doh</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>Tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立来</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>Fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>灾难</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>普天下</rb><rt>pʽu-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>时疫</rb><rt>z-yüoh</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>除灭掉</rb><rt>djü-mih-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>兴起来</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>遍天下</rb><rt>pin-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>扬开</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名声</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>骄</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>傲</rb><rt>ngao</rt><rb>阻隔</rb><rt>tsu-kah</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>重个</rb><rt>djong-go</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开国</rb><rt>kʽæ-koh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>收进</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所有个</rb><rt>sô-yiu-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田头</rb><rt>din-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>收进</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>屋里个</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」』」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>伸出</rb><rt>Sing-cʽih</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>蔬菜个</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ-go</rt><rb>上顶</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>发出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>雷</rb><rt>le</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>火光</rb><rt>ho-kwông</rt><rb>闪来</rb><rt>sin-læ</rt><rb>闪去</rb><rt>sin-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>夹勒</rb><rt>kah-leh</rt><rb>火光</rb><rt>ho-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怕势势个</rb><rt>pʽô-shü-shü-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>开国</rb><rt>kʽæ-koh</rt><rb>以来</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田里个</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>打掉</rb><rt>tang-diao</rt><rb>田里个</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拗断</rb><rt>ao-dön</rt><rb>田里个</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>树木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>庉间个</rb><rt>deng-kæn-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>歌珊</rb><rt>Ko-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恶个</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>够了</rb><rt>keo-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>雷</rb><rt>le</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雷</rb><rt>le</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>麻</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大麦</rb><rt>do-mah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>打掉了</rb><rt>tang-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>大麦</rb><rt>do-mah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>结佩</rb><rt>kyih-be</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>麻</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>开花了</rb><rt>kʽæ-hwô-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>小麦</rb><rt>siao-mah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>粗麦</rb><rt>tsʽu-mah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>打掉</rb><rt>tang-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>发出来</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>dzing-nga</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雷</rb><rt>le</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歇</rb><rt>hyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雨</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>倒落</rb><rt>tao-loh</rt><rb>地里</rb><rt>di-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>雨</rb><rt>yü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>雷</rb><rt>le</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>歇了</rb><rt>hyih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。
