### C. X.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>臣子个</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所做个</rb><rt>sô-tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>显出个</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-tô</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>低声</rb><rt>ti-sing</rt><rb>小意</rb><rt>siao-i</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>蝗虫</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>面顶</rb><rt>min-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>看弗见</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>坏掉个</rb><rt>wæ-diao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>树木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>充满</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>臣子个</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>出世</rb><rt>cʽih-si</rt><rb>以来</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>看见过个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-go</rt></ruby>。』」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>灭掉了</rb><rt>mih-diao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗晓得</rb><rt>feh-hyiao-teh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>老老</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>少少</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>保佑</rb><rt>pao-yiu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前了</rb><rt>min-zin-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>弗是介</rb><rt>Feh-z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求个</rb><rt>gyiu-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>伸出</rb><rt>Sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>蝗虫</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>剩落东个</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>东风</rb><rt>tong-fong</rt><rb>整日</rb><rt>tsing-nyih</rt><rb>整夜</rb><rt>tsing-yia</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>东风</rb><rt>tong-fong</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>蝗虫</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>蝗虫</rb><rt>Wông-djong</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>遍地</rb><rt>pin-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>停来</rb><rt>ding-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从前</rb><rt>dzong-zin</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>蝗虫</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>箇种介个</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>蝗虫</rb><rt>Wông-djong</rt><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>到处</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>地面顶</rb><rt>di-min-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>黑暗个</rb><rt>heh-en-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>剩落东</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong</rt><rb>田里个</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>树里个</rb><rt>jü-li-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>吃了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>树里</rb><rt>jü-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>田里个</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>蔬菜</rb><rt>su-tsʽæ</rt><rb>青青个</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽing-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>召拢</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>宽恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>免</rb><rt>min</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇遭个</rb><rt>keh-tsao-go</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发起</rb><rt>fah-kyʽi</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>西风</rb><rt>si-fong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>蝗虫</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>吹进</rb><rt>cʽü-tsing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>红</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>蝗虫</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-loh</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只好</rb><rt>tsih-hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>摸来摸去</rb><rt>moh-læ-moh-kyʽi</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>墨漆地暗</rb><rt>moh-tsʽeh-di-en</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>看弗见</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>庉处个</rb><rt>deng-cʽü-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>福礼</rb><rt>foh-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>蹄子</rb><rt>di-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>剩落东</rb><rt>dzing-loh-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗晓得</rb><rt>feh-hyiao-teh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>见面个</rb><rt>kyin-min-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh wô*, Keh heh-en ʽeo-ʽeo jih-jih, tsing-ziang moh-leh-djoh go. 或话，箇黑暗厚厚实实，正像摸勒着个。（原注）</sup>

---

### C. XI.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>降</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>灾难</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>个个</rb><rt>ko-ko</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>个个</rb><rt>ko-ko</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>金银个</rb><rt>kying-nying-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>贵重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>半夜过</rb><rt>Pun-yia-ko</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>坐来</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>座位里</rb><rt>zo-we-li</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>牵磨个</rb><rt>kyʽin-mo-go</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>各到处</rb><rt>koh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>号泪大哭</rb><rt>ʽao-li-da-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>箇种介个</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>狗</rb><rt>keo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>动</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>舌头</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>分别</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>伏带倒</rb><rt>boh-tæn-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>大怏</rb><rt>do-ông</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. XII.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>正月</rb><rt>tsing-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>年终</rb><rt>nyin-cong</rt><rb>月份个</rb><rt>yüih-veng-go</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>初日</rb><rt>tsʽu-jih</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>拣</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>宗族</rb><rt>tsong-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>份份</rb><rt>veng-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>份份</rb><rt>veng-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>拣</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>杀仔</rb><rt>sah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>逾越节个</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一束</rb><rt>ih-shoh</rt><rb>牛膝草</rb><rt>ngeo-sih-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>浸勒</rb><rt>tsing-leh</rt><rb>碗里个</rb><rt>un-li-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>碗里个</rb><rt>un-li-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>搽来</rb><rt>djô-læ</rt><rb>门个</rb><rt>meng-go</rt><rb>楣</rb><rt>mi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>两边个</rb><rt>liang-pin-go</rt><rb>敦枋里</rb><rt>teng-fông-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗许</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋里个</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>走过去</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一看见</rb><rt>ih-kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>门个</rb><rt>meng-go</rt><rb>楣</rb><rt>mi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>两边个</rb><rt>liang-pin-go</rt><rb>敦枋里</rb><rt>teng-fông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>跳过</rb><rt>tʽiao-ko</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗许</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>抄灭个</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>*s-tsʽa*</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>永远个</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>礼节</rb><rt>li-tsih</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>逾越节个</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>跳过</rb><rt>tʽiao-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。』」<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>讴带倒</rb><rt>eo-tæn-tao</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>半夜过</rb><rt>pun-yia-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>坐来</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>座位里</rb><rt>zo-we-li</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>监里个</rb><rt>kæn-li-go</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>号泪大哭</rb><rt>ʽao-li-da-kʽoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祝</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>福</rb><rt>foh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>催</rb><rt>tsʽe</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>呒酵水</rb><rt>m-kao-shü</rt><rb>𢫭好了个</rb><rt>nyüoh-hao-liao-go</rt><rb>面粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>𢫭粉个</rb><rt>nyüoh-feng-go</rt><rb>面盘</rb><rt>min-bun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>包勒</rb><rt>pao-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>肩胛头</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>兰塞</rb><rt>Læn-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搬过</rb><rt>pun-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>疏割</rb><rt>Su-keh</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>步行个</rb><rt>bu-ʽang-go</rt><rb>大约</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六十万</rb><rt>loh-jih-væn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>夹杂拢的个</rb><rt>kah-dzeh-long-tih-go</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带出来个</rb><rt>ta-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>𢫭好了</rb><rt>nyüoh-hao-liao</rt><rb>面粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>烹</rb><rt>pʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>呒酵水个</rb><rt>m-kao-shü-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>面粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt><rb>呒酵水</rb><rt>m-kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗能够</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>宽缓</rb><rt>kwʽun-wun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>粮食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>备好</rb><rt>be-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>庉来</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>满了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>兵队</rb><rt>ping-de</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>逾越节个</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耽搁东个</rb><rt>tæn-koh-tong-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>买来个</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>外国</rb><rt>Nga-koh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>雇来个</rb><rt>kwu-læ-go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一间</rb><rt>ih-kæn</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>拗断</rb><rt>ao-dön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一梗</rb><rt>ih-kwang</rt><rb>骨头</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>合公会</rb><rt>ʽeh-kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>出门人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>各样</rb><rt>koh-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>呒</rb><rt>m</rt><rb>周割礼个</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li-go</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>本地</rb><rt>Peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央个</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>出门人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>兵队</rb><rt>ping-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. XIII.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>记念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆个</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>走出个</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>能干个</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>酵水个</rb><rt>kao-shü-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚笔</rb><rt>Üô-pih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>月里</rb><rt>yüih-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>流</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蜜糖个</rb><rt>mih-dông-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>礼节</rb><rt>li-tsih</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>七日</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>呒酵水个</rb><rt>m-kao-shü-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>七日</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>呒酵水个</rb><rt>m-kao-shü-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>酵水个</rb><rt>kao-shü-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>记号</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>记认</rb><rt>kyi-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>额角</rb><rt>ngah-koh</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>能干个</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>每年</rb><rt>me-nyin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>拗断</rb><rt>ao-dön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>头颈</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>头一胎个</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ-go</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>能干个</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆个</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>硬心</rb><rt>ngang-sing</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>头一胎个</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>头一胎个</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>长子</rb><rt>tsiang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>记号</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>额角</rb><rt>ngah-koh</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>能干个</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非利士</rb><rt>Fi-li-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>近</rb><rt>gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>Kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>打仗个</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>懊悔</rb><rt>ao-hwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>走归</rb><rt>tseo-kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>转弯</rb><rt>cün-w̆æn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>红</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>摆勒</rb><rt>pa-leh</rt><rb>阵势</rb><rt>dzing-shü</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约瑟</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>骨头</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>Tsiang-læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照顾</rb><rt>tsiao-kwu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>骨头</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>疏割</rb><rt>Su-keh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到勒</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>隔壁</rb><rt>kah-pih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以藤</rb><rt>Yi-deng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>日里</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>云个</rb><rt>yüing-go</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>火个</rb><rt>ho-go</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>日里</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt><rb>云柱</rb><rt>yüing-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>火柱</rb><rt>ho-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>隐落</rb><rt>ing-loh</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. XIV.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弯转</rb><rt>w̆æn-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比哈希录</rb><rt>Pi-ha-hyi-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>红</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>密夺</u>个</rb><rt>Mih-deh-go</rt><rb>中间</rb><rt>cong-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力洗分</rb><rt>Pô-lih-si-feng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>对面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>对头</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>迷路</rb><rt>mi-lu</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>围困了</rb><rt>we-kwʽeng-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>追赶</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>兵队</rb><rt>ping-de</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>折转</rb><rt>coh-cün</rt><rb>攻击</rb><rt>kong-kyih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>？」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带领</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>六百</rb><rt>loh-pah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>带领官</rb><rt>ta-ling-kwun</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>追赶</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>散脚</rb><rt>sæn-kyiah</rt><rb>散手</rb><rt>sæn-siu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>马</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>马兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>步兵</rb><rt>bu-ping</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>追赶</rb><rt>tse-ken</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>比哈希录</rb><rt>Pi-ha-hyi-loh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>巴力洗分</u>个</rb><rt>Pô-lih-si-feng-go</rt><rb>对面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帐篷个</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赶着</rb><rt>ken-djoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>近拢来了</rb><rt>gying-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>坟地</rb><rt>veng-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>岂弗是</rb><rt>kyʽi-feh-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>吵扰</rb><rt>tsʽao-ziao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>静静</rb><rt>zing-zing</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh wô*, Sæn go nying. 或话，三个人。（原注）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>Eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>上顶</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>燥路</rb><rt>sao-lu</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>追上来</rb><rt>tse-zông-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>兵队</rb><rt>ping-de</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>骑马个</rb><rt>gyi-mô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>马兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>走个</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趱过去</rb><rt>tsæn-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>后背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>云柱</rb><rt>yüing-djü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>趱过去</rb><rt>tsæn-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立来</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>后背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>，<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>营寨</rb><rt>ying-za</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营寨个</rb><rt>ying-za-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>整夜</rb><rt>tsing-yia</rt><rb>近弗拢</rb><rt>gying-feh-long</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>上顶</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>整夜</rb><rt>tsing-yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>猛猛</rb><rt>mang-mang</rt><rb>东风</rb><rt>tong-fong</rt><rb>拉住了</rb><rt>lah-djü-leh</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>燥地</rb><rt>sao-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>燥路</rb><rt>sao-lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>左右</rb><rt>tso-yiu</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>墙</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>敦起了</rb><rt>teng-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>赶上去</rb><rt>ken-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>马</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>马兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>快亮</rb><rt>kwʽa-liang</rt><rb>箇更</rb><rt>keh-kang</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火柱</rb><rt>ho-djü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>云柱</rb><rt>yüing-djü</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>兵队</rb><rt>ping-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>散乱</rb><rt>sæn-lön</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>埃及</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih-go</rt><rb>行伍</rb><rt>ʽông-wu</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>脱掉</rb><rt>Tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>车子个</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts-go</rt><rb>轮盘</rb><rt>leng-bun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt><rb>避过</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打仗了</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>上顶</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>合拢来</rb><rt>ʽeh-long-læ</rt><rb>浸没</rb><rt>tsing-mah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>马兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup>
