<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>种</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>山里个</rb><rt>sæn-li-go</rt><rb>基业</rb><rt>kyi-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>备办个</rb><rt>be-bæn-go</rt><rb>庉处</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>建造个</rb><rt>kyin-zao-go</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>！<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>马</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>车子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>马兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>盖住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>燥地</rb><rt>sao-di</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh wô*, Ting yiu yüong-wô. 或话，顶有荣华。（原注）</sup>

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>阿姊</rb><rt>ah-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>摇顿鼓</rb><rt>yiao-teng-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>摇顿鼓</rb><rt>yiao-teng-kwu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跳舞</rb><rt>tʽiao-vu</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>米利暗</rb><rt>Mi-li-en</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>应起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

「<ruby><rb>歌唱</rb><rt>Ko-tsʽông</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>得胜</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>马</rb><rt>Mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>骑马个</rb><rt>gyi-mô-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-loh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh wô*, Ting yiu yüong-wô. 或话，顶有荣华。（原注）</sup>

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>红</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>书珥</rb><rt>Shü-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野里</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>旷野里</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt><rb>走了</rb><rt>tseo-leh</rt><rb>三日</rb><rt>sæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>Tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>玛拉</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-lah-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>吃弗来</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-feh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt><rb>苦</rb><rt>kwʽu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛拉</rb><rt>Mô-lah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-loh</rt><rb>水里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>甜了</rb><rt>din-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>病痛</rb><rt>bing-tʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>病个</rb><rt>bing-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Kʽwu *ka-shih*. 就是「苦」解说。（原注）</sup>

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以琳</rb><rt>Yi-ling</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>𡍲</rb><rt>tʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>枣子</rb><rt>tsao-ts</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>株</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>近</rb><rt>gying</rt><rb>水个</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。

---

### C. XVI.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以琳</rb><rt>Yi-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>二月</rb><rt>nyi-yüih</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汛</rb><rt>Sing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以琳</rb><rt>Yi-ling</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>中间</rb><rt>cong-kyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野里</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>倒弗如</rb><rt>tao-feh-jü</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>死来</rb><rt>si-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>坐来</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt><rb>肉碗</rb><rt>nyüoh-un</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>粮食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>吃饱</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>饿煞</rb><rt>ngo-sah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>粮食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>收进</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>逐日</rb><rt>djoh-nyih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第六</rb><rt>di-loh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>收进个</rb><rt>siu-tsing-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>预备</rb><rt>yü-be</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>收进个</rb><rt>siu-tsing-go</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>一倍</rb><rt>ih-be</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>吃饱</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>怨恨个</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng-go</rt><rb>说话了</rb><rt>shih-wô-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>Ming-tsiao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>圣日</rb><rt>sing-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>煮个</rb><rt>ts-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>煮</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>烹个</rb><rt>pʽang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>烹</rb><rt>pʽang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>余多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>囥东</rb><rt>kʽông-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>留到</rb><rt>liu-tao</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>留到</rb><rt>liu-tao</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>臭</rb><rt>tsʽiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>虫</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>第六</rb><rt>di-loh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>两日个</rb><rt>liang-nyih-go</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>koh-nying</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>庉来</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>是介个</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>安息了</rb><rt>en-sih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家</rb><rt>keh-kô</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>吗嗱</rb><rt>Mô-nô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>芫荽子</rb><rt>nyün-se-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>白白个</rb><rt>bah-bah-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>蜜糖</rb><rt>mih-dông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>扁饼</rb><rt>pin-ping</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>𣥼满勒</rb><rt>tsi-mun-leh</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>阿吗</rb><rt>ah-mô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>囥东</rb><rt>kʽông-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国个</rb><rt>koh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>养</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>瓶</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>阿吗<sup> </sup>个</rb><rt>ah-mô-go</rt><rb>吗嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放来</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>囥东</rb><rt>kʽông-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放来</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>约箱个</rb><rt>iah-siang-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>囥东</rb><rt>kʽông-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>吗嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>吗嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦南</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>阿吗</rb><rt>Ah-mô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>以法<sup> </sup>个</rb><rt>yi-fah-go</rt><rb>十股</rb><rt>jih-kwu</rt><rb>之</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Liang-sing. 就是两升。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Kʽen* 16 tsih. 看 16 节。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[3\] *Ziu-z* Soh-go tong-si. *Kʽen* 15 tsih. 就是「啥个东西」。看 15 节。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[4\] *Ziu-z* Liang-teo. 就是两斗。（原注）</sup>

### C. XVII.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>汛</u>个</rb><rt>Sing-go</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哩非定</rb><rt>Li-fi-ding</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥干</rb><rt>soh-ken</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>口渴煞</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh-sah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>几几乎</rb><rt>kyi-kyi-wu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>河个</rb><rt>ʽo-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>立来</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>何烈</rb><rt>ʽO-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛撒</rb><rt>Mô-sah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利巴</rb><rt>Mi-li-pô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* S-s. 就是「试试」。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z* Tsang-leng. 就是「争论」。（原注）</sup>

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哩非定</rb><rt>Li-fi-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>拐杖</rb><rt>kwa-dziang</rt><rb>立来</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>山顶头</rb><rt>sæn-ting-den</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>户珥</rb><rt>Wu-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山顶头</rb><rt>sæn-ting-den</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赢了</rb><rt>ying-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>宕落</rb><rt>dang-loh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赢了</rb><rt>ying-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>吃力</rb><rt>kyʽih-lih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放来</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>下头</rb><rt>ʽô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>坐的</rb><rt>zo-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>户珥</rb><rt>Wu-r</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挡</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>摇动</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>落山</rb><rt>loh-sæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt><rb>杀败</rb><rt>sah-ba</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>载落</rb><rt>tsæ-loh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一本</rb><rt>ih-peng</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>记认</rb><rt>kyi-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>读</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚玛力</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih-go</rt><rb>记号</rb><rt>kyi-ʽao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>涂销</rb><rt>du-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尼西</rb><rt>Nyi-si</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚玛力</rb><rt>Üô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-wô*, Læ keh peng shü-li. 或话，来箇本书里。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z* Yiæ-ʽo-wô ngô gyi-ʽao. 就是「耶和华我旗号」。（原注）</sup>

---

### C. XVIII.

<ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>MO-SI-GO</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叶罗</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做过个</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>Zin-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西坡拉</rb><rt>Si-pʽo-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叶罗</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革顺</rb><rt>Keh-jing</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>外国</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出门人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以列撒</rb><rt>Yi-lih-sah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>宝剑</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叶罗</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>旷野里</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帐篷个</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叶罗</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>作揖</rb><rt>tsoh-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>亲</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘴</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>问问</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>帐篷里</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做拨</rb><rt>tso-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>遭着个</rb><rt>tsao-djoh-go</rt><rb>难头</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>叶罗</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做拨</rb><rt>tso-peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>叶罗</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>祝谢</rb><rt>Coh-zia</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>骄傲</rb><rt>kyiao-ngao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>赢</rb><rt>ying</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叶罗</rb><rt>Yih-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>夜头</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt><rb>立来</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>独自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>夜头</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt><rb>立来</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>问问</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>两下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>法度</rb><rt>fah-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>告向</rb><rt>kao-hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>忒</rb><rt>tʽeh</rt><rb>重头</rb><rt>djong-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗能够</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>独自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>痿痿碎碎</rb><rt>w̆e-w̆e-se-se</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>法度</rb><rt>fah-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>走个</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>才能个</rb><rt>dzæ-neng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>诚诚实实个</rb><rt>dzing-dzing-jih-jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>贪赃个</rb><rt>tʽen-tsông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>之上</rb><rt>ts-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十个</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>随时</rb><rt>ze-z</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>转</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>小个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>轻可</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>担头</rb><rt>tæn-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>担子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>挑勒动</rb><rt>tʽiao-leh-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安安稳稳</rb><rt>en-en-w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>庉处</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈人个</rb><rt>dziang-nying-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>才能个</rb><rt>dzæ-neng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>之</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十个</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>随时</rb><rt>ze-z</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>转</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>小个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Jing-ming pông-dzu ngô. 就是「神明帮助我」。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 垂头丧气。《字语汇解》记 w̆e-w̆e'-ze-ze' 痿痿瘁°瘁°</sup>

### C. XIX.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>三月</rb><rt>sæn-yüih</rt><rb>初一</rb><rt>tsʽu-ih</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哩非定</rb><rt>Li-fi-ding</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扎营</rb><rt>tsah-ying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田畈里</rb><rt>din-pæn-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>山面前</rb><rt>sæn-min-zin</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家</rb><rt>keh-kô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup> 『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>老鹰</rb><rt>lao-ing</rt><rb>翼梢</rb><rt>yih-sao</rt><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>列国</rb><rt>lih-koh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>宝贝</rb><rt>pao-pe</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>全地</rb><rt>djün-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>当面</rb><rt>tông-min</rt><rb>传拨</rb><rt>djün-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>合口齐动</rb><rt>ʽeh-kʽeo-zi-dong</rt><rb>应起来</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>回报</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>预备</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>一只</rb><rt>Ih-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djoh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>箭</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>射</rb><rt>zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗能够</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>号筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>吹勒</rb><rt>cʽü-leh</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>方才</rb><rt>fông-dzæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>山边沿</rb><rt>sæn-pin-yin</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>14</sup>
