### C. XX.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆个</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>Djü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>泥塑木雕</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>地底下</rb><rt>di-ti-ʽô</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嫉邪个</rb><rt>dzih-zia-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>三四</rb><rt>sæn-s</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩</rb><rt>eng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>妄</rb><rt>vông</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>妄</rb><rt>vông</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>六日</rb><rt>Loh-nyih</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>工</rb><rt>kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>凡百个</rb><rt>væn-pah-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>Keh-nyih</rt><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>尔自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>里向个</rb><rt>li-hyiang-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>六日</rb><rt>loh-nyih</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>造成</rb><rt>zao-dzing</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里向个</rb><rt>li-hyiang-go</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>祝赞</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>偷盗</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>谎告</rb><rt>hwông-kao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>看相</rb><rt>kʽen-siang</rt><rb>邻舍家个</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>看相</rb><rt>kʽen-siang</rt><rb>邻舍家个</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>驴</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>邻舍家个</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>看相</rb><rt>kʽen-siang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>响雷</rb><rt>hyiang-le</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>闪电</rb><rt>sin-din</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>号筒个</rb><rt>ʽao-dong-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>发出个</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-go</rt><rb>烟</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>退落</rb><rt>tʽe-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>落来</rb><rt>loh-læ</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>威光</rb><rt>w̆e-kwông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>烂泥个</rb><rt>na-nyi-go</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>坛上</rb><rt>dæn-zông</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>谢恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>名头个</rb><rt>ming-deo-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djoh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福</rb><rt>foh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>石头个</rb><rt>zah-deo-go</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>笃出个</rb><rt>toh-cʽih-go</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>凿过个</rb><rt>zoh-ko-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>格步阶</rb><rt>kah-bu-kæn</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>坛里</rb><rt>dæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>露出</rb><rt>lu-cʽih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

---

### C. XXI.

「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>NG</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>单身</rb><rt>tæn-sing</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>单身</rb><rt>tæn-sing</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>儿囡了</rb><rt>ng-nô-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>家小</rb><rt>kô-siao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>单身</rb><rt>tæn-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>家小</rb><rt>kô-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>门个</rb><rt>meng-go</rt><rb>敦枋沿</rb><rt>teng-fông-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>钻子</rb><rt>cün-ts</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-tô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>一生</rb><rt>ih-sang</rt><rb>一世</rb><rt>ih-si</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-tsia* *fæn* Z-s. 或者翻士师。</sup>

<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>弗欢喜</rb><rt>feh-hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>外国</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>减落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>衣穿</rb><rt>i-cʽün</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>情分</rb><rt>dzing-veng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三样</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>打煞</rb><rt>Tang-sah</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>故意</rb><rt>kwu-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>托付</rb><rt>tʽoh-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手里个</rb><rt>siu-li-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>故意</rb><rt>kwu-i</rt><rb>谋杀</rb><rt>meo-sah</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>祭坛里</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抲去</rb><rt>kʽô-kyʽi</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>Tang</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>阿娘个</rb><rt>ah-nyiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>拐勒</rb><rt>Kwa-leh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>卖其掉</rb><rt>ma-gyi-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djoh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>咒骂</rb><rt>Tsiu-mô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>阿娘个</rb><rt>ah-nyiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>争造</rb><rt>tsang-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>拳头</rb><rt>gyün-deo</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>困来</rb><rt>kwʽeng-læ</rt><rb>眠床里</rb><rt>min-zông-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠勒</rb><rt>kʽao-leh</rt><rb>哀杖</rb><rt>æ-dziang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>听还</rb><rt>tʽing-wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>费事失业个</rb><rt>fi-z-shih-nyih-go</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>两日</rb><rt>liang-nyih</rt><rb>弗死</rb><rt>feh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相打</rb><rt>siang-tang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>触犯</rb><rt>cʽoh-væn</rt><rb>双身</rb><rt>shông-sing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>堕身</rb><rt>do-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>伤害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>士师</rb><rt>z-s</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一命</rb><rt>ih-ming</rt><rb>抵</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>一命</rb><rt>ih-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>Ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抵还</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>抵还</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>抵还</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>抵还</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>火烫</rb><rt>ho-tʽông</rt><rb>抵还</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>火烫</rb><rt>ho-tʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伤痕</rb><rt>sông-ʽeng</rt><rb>抵还</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>伤痕</rb><rt>sông-ʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拷打</rb><rt>kʽao-tang</rt><rb>抵还</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>拷打</rb><rt>kʽao-tang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>奴仆个</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>使女个</rb><rt>s-nyü-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>坏掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>打落</rb><rt>tang-loh</rt><rb>奴仆个</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>使女个</rb><rt>s-nyü-go</rt><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>𧤼着</rb><rt>gyüih-djoh</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>𧤼煞</rb><rt>gyüih-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗吃</rb><rt>feh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>牛个</rb><rt>ngeo-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>呒罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𧤼</rb><rt>gyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>关</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>𧤼煞</rb><rt>gyüih-sah</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>取赎个</rb><rt>cʽü-joh-go</rt><rb>价钿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>所定个</rb><rt>sô-ding-go</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>𧤼</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>人家个</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>𧤼</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>人家个</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>例</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>办法</rb><rt>bæn-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>Ngeo</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>𧤼</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>人家个</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>*z-kʽeh*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>𢳚开</rb><rt>hyiao-kʽæ</rt><rb>井盖</rb><rt>tsing-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>盖好</rb><rt>kæ-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>陷落</rb><rt>ʽæn-loh</rt><rb>井里</rb><rt>tsing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>箇口</rb><rt>keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>赔还</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt><rb>牲口个</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>𧤼煞</rb><rt>gyüih-sah</rt><rb>别人个</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𧤼</rb><rt>gyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>关</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>赔</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。」

### C. XXII.

「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ZIAH</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>五头</rb><rt>ng-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>赔</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>一头</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>四只</rb><rt>s-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>赔</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>碰见</rb><rt>pʽong-kyin</rt><rb>盗贼</rb><rt>dao-zeh</rt><rb>挖</rb><rt>w̆eh</rt><rb>墙洞</rb><rt>ziang-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>煞</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>填命</rb><rt>din-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>填命</rb><rt>din-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>贼赃</rb><rt>zah-tsông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>赔还个</rb><rt>be-wæn-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>赔</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>偷盗</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>偷个</rb><rt>tʽeo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>赔还</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人家个</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>葡萄园</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>吃掉</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>人家个</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>葡萄园里个</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li-go</rt><rb>顶好个</rb><rt>ting-hao-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>赔还</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>飞散</rb><rt>fi-sæn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>刺棚里</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>延开</rb><rt>yin-kʽæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>对间个</rb><rt>te-kæn-go</rt><rb>麦里</rb><rt>mah-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>田稻</rb><rt>din-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>田里个</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>麦</rb><rt>mah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>起火个</rb><rt>kyʽi-ho-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>赔还</rb><rt>be-Wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>货物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>代管</rb><rt>dæ-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>偷去</rb><rt>tʽeo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>贼</rb><rt>zeh</rt><rb>一抲着</rb><rt>ih-kʽô-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>赔还</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>抲弗着</rb><rt>kʽô-feh-djoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>审问</rb><rt>sing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>邻舍家个</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>货物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>疏失个</rb><rt>su-shih-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>两下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定罪个</rb><rt>ding-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *fæn* Z-s. 或翻士师。</sup>

<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽoh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>代管</rb><rt>dæ-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>伤</rb><rt>sông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>偷去</rb><rt>tʽeo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒</rb><rt>m</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>两下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>邻舍家个</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>赔还</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>偷去</rb><rt>tʽeo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>赔还</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>踏煞</rb><rt>dah-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>赔</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>踏煞个</rb><rt>dah-sah-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>借来个</rb><rt>tsia-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>伤</rb><rt>sông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>赔</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>赔还</rb><rt>be-wæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>贳来个</rb><rt>shü-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>贳钿</rb><rt>shü-din</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>引诱</rb><rt>ying-yiu</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>许配个</rb><rt>hyü-pʽe-go</rt><rb>童女</rb><rt>dong-nyü</rt><rb>同房</rb><rt>dong-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>聘定</rb><rt>pʽing-ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>决定</rb><rt>kyüih-ding</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>称</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聘定</rb><rt>pʽing-ding</rt><rb>大姑娘个</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>邪术个</rb><rt>zia-jih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> 「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众生</rb><rt>cong-sang</rt><rb>同床个</rb><rt>dong-zông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> 「<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>专门</rb><rt>cün-meng</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>jing-ming-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> 「<ruby><rb>出门人</rb><rt>Cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>吵扰</rb><rt>tsʽao-ziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出门人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>孤儿</rb><rt>Kwu-r</rt><rb>寡妇</rb><rt>kwô-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>生火</rb><rt>sang-ho</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>寡妇</rb><rt>kwô-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>孤儿</rb><rt>kwu-r</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>穷</rb><rt>gyüong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>借</rb><rt>tsia</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>贪利个</rb><rt>tʽen-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>利钿</rb><rt>li-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>邻舍家个</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>当头</rb><rt>tông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>落山个</rb><rt>loh-sæn-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身底个</rb><rt>sing-ti-go</rt><rb>遮盖</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>皮肤个</rb><rt>bi-fu-go</rt><rb>穿着</rb><rt>cʽün-tsiah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>困</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>慈悲个</rb><rt>dz-pe-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>谤毁</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>咒骂</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>官长</rb><rt>kwun-tsiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>尝新个</rb><rt>zông-sing-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>献上来</rb><rt>hyin-zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>迟缓</rb><rt>dzi-wun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>头一胎个</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第八</rb><rt>di-pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>圣洁个</rb><rt>sing-kyih-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>咬伤个</rb><rt>ngao-sông-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>狗</rb><rt>keo</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

---

### C. XXIII.

「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>NG</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>谣言</rb><rt>yiao-yin</rt><rb>传开去</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>恶人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt><rb>绞手</rb><rt>gao-siu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>大众</rb><rt>da-cong</rt><rb>作恶</rb><rt>tsoh-ôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>徇</rb><rt>shing</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>情面</rb><rt>dzing-min</rt><rb>冤枉</rb><rt>ün-wông</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>包庇</rb><rt>pao-pi</rt><rb>穷人个</rb><rt>gyüong-nying-go</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djoh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>仇敌个</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>牵转来</rb><rt>kyʽin-cün-læ</rt><rb>归还</rb><rt>kwe-wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>揹弗动</rb><rt>pe-feh-dong</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>弗情愿</rb><rt>feh-dzing-nyün</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必巧勒</rb><rt>pih-kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>穷人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>离远</rb><rt>li-yün</rt><rb>假个</rb><rt>kô-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>呒罪个</rb><rt>m-ze-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>恶人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>贿赂</rb><rt>hwe-lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>贿赂</rb><rt>hwe-lu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>瞎</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>弯曲</rb><rt>w̆æn-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>吵扰</rb><rt>tsʽao-ziao</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>出门人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>六</rb><rt>Loh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>种</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>出产</rb><rt>cʽih-tsʽæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歇落</rb><rt>hyih-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>空过</rb><rt>kʽong-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>穷</rb><rt>gyüong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>剩落个</rb><rt>dzing-loh-go</rt><rb>多头</rb><rt>to-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>田里个</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>橄榄</rb><rt>ken-læn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>葡萄园</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>六日</rb><rt>Loh-nyih</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驴</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>使女个</rb><rt>s-nyü-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>出门人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>张扬</rb><rt>tsiang-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>当心</rb><rt>tông-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>出口</rb><rt>cʽih-kʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>祭物个</rb><rt>tsi-veh-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>和勒</rb><rt>ʽo-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>酵水个</rb><rt>kao-shü-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>献上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>祭物个</rb><rt>tsi-veh-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>留到</rb><rt>liu-tao</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>结个</rb><rt>kyih-go</rt><rb>尝新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>殿里</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>煮</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿娘个</rb><rt>ah-nyiang-go</rt><rb>奶里</rb><rt>na-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>打发</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>S-tsʽa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>保护</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>备办个</rb><rt>be-bæn-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>S-tsʽa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚摩利</rb><rt>Üô-mo-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>比利洗</rb><rt>Pi-li-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶布士</rb><rt>Yiæ-pu-z</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份个</rb><rt>dzoh-veng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>榜样</rb><rt>pông-yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拆毁</rb><rt>tsʽah-hwe</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-loh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>威严</rb><rt>w̆e-nyin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吵乱</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>转</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>背</rb><rt>pe</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>黄蜂</rb><rt>wông-fong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赶走</rb><rt>ken-tseo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希未</rb><rt>Hyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>赫</rb><rt>Heh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-lah</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>荒掉</rb><rt>hwông-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>茂盛</rb><rt>meo-zing</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>兴旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>址界</rb><rt>ts-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>红</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非利士</rb><rt>Fi-li-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大河</rb><rt>do-ʽo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立约</rb><rt>lih-iah</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>庉来</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>引诱</rb><rt>ying-yiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>陷</rb><rt>ʽæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>圈套里</rb><rt>kyʽün-tʽao-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Pah-lah ʽÔ. 就是<u>伯拉</u>河。（原注）</sup>

---

### C. XXIV.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拿答</rb><rt>Nô-tah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比户</rb><rt>Üô-pi-wu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>七十个</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>亲近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走上来</rb><rt>tseo-zông-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>合口齐动</rb><rt>ʽeh-kʽeo-zi-dong</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所话个</rb><rt>sô-wô-go</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>写落</rb><rt>sia-loh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>早天亮</rb><rt>tsao-tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山下</rb><rt>sæn-ʽô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>根</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>后生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>谢恩</rb><rt>zia-eng</rt><rb>祭个</rb><rt>tsi-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>𣥼来</rb><rt>tsi-læ</rt><rb>盘</rb><rt>bun</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>扎来</rb><rt>tsah-læ</rt><rb>坛里</rb><rt>dæn-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>立约个</rb><rt>lih-iah-go</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>读</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所话个</rb><rt>sô-wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>扎来</rb><rt>tsah-læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立约个</rb><rt>lih-iah-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>设立个</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>拿答</rb><rt>Nô-tah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比户</rb><rt>Üô-pi-wu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>七十个</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>走上去</rb><rt>tseo-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>青青个</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽing-go</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>铺的</rb><rt>pʽu-tih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>体质</rb><rt>tʽi-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>梗青</rb><rt>kwang-tsʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>尊贵</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-loh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>石头个</rb><rt>zah-deo-go</rt><rb>碑牌</rb><rt>pe-ba</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写个</rb><rt>sia-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>办事</rb><rt>bæn-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约书亚</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>庉东</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>户珥</rb><rt>Wu-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>遮住了</rb><rt>tsô-djü-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>庉来</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>六日</rb><rt>loh-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>云个</rb><rt>yüing-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt><rb>耶和华个</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山顶头</rb><rt>sæn-ting-den</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>dziah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉来</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>。

### C. XXV.

<ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>甘心</rb><rt>ken-sing</rt><rb>情愿个</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>接受个</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>银</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>铜</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>；<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>蓝</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>红</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>细麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>；<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>点亮个</rb><rt>tin-liang-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>傅个</rb><rt>fu-go</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>香气个</rb><rt>hyiang-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>；<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>碧玉</rb><rt>pih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>嵌来</rb><rt>kʽæn-læ</rt><rb>以弗</rb><rt>Yi-feh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>胸牌里个</rb><rt>Hyüong-ba-li-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看个</rb><rt>kʽen-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>帐篷个</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>样式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>器皿个</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming-go</rt><rb>样式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。

### C. XXVII.

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>廿根</rb><rt>nyiæn-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>铜</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>廿个</rb><rt>nyiæn-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>柱子个</rb><rt>djü-ts-go</rt><rb>钩子</rb><rt>keo-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>横档</rb><rt>wang-tông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幔帐</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>阿买</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>廿根</rb><rt>nyiæn-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>铜</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>廿个</rb><rt>nyiæn-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>柱子个</rb><rt>djü-ts-go</rt><rb>钩子</rb><rt>keo-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>横档</rb><rt>wang-tông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt><rb>围墙</rb><rt>yü-ziang</rt><rb>阔个</rb><rt>kwʽeh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>幔帐</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>阿买</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>十根</rb><rt>jih-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>十个</rb><rt>jih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>围墙</rb><rt>yü-ziang</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>阔</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>阿买</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>荡边个</rb><rt>Dông-pin-go</rt><rb>幔帐</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>阿买</rb><rt>ah-ma</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>三根</rb><rt>sæn-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇边个</rb><rt>Keh-pin-go</rt><rb>幔帐</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>阿买</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>三根</rb><rt>sæn-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>围墙个</rb><rt>yü-ziang-go</rt><rb>门帘</rb><rt>meng-lin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>廿</rb><rt>nyiæn</rt><rb>阿买</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>绣花</rb><rt>siu-hwô</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>蓝</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>红</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>细麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt><rb>四根</rb><rt>s-keng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>四个</rb><rt>s-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>围墙</rb><rt>Yü-ziang</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>柱子</rb><rt>djü-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>横档</rb><rt>wang-tông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>银</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>钩子</rb><rt>keo-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>铜</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>围墙</rb><rt>Yü-ziang</rt><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>阿买</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阔</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>阿买</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿买</rb><rt>*ah-ma*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>幔帐</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>细麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>铜个</rb><rt>dong-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>帐篷里</rb><rt>Tsiang-bong-li</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>钉子</rb><rt>ting-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>围墙个</rb><rt>yü-ziang-go</rt><rb>钉子</rb><rt>ting-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>铜</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Kʽen* C. 26. 2. 看 C. 26. 2.（原注）</sup>

<sup>20</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>清洁个</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih-go</rt><rb>橄榄</rb><rt>ken-læn</rt><rb>舂</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>灯盏</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt><rb>时刻</rb><rt>z-kʽeh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>点</rb><rt>tin</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>约箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>幔帐</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>夜头</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>整顿</rb><rt>tsing-teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>永远个</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>。」
