### C. XXVIII.

「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>NG</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拿答</rb><rt>Nô-tah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚比户</rb><rt>Üô-pi-wu</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以利亚撒</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以大玛</rb><rt>Yi-da-mô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>艳丽</rb><rt>yin-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>充满</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>智慧</rb><rt>cü-we</rt><rb>灵性个</rb><rt>ling-sing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>胸牌</rb><rt>Hyüong-ba</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>以弗</rb><rt>Yi-feh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>套衣</rb><rt>Tʽao-i</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>花衣</rb><rt>Hwô-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>帽</rb><rt>Mao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>Ta</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>蓝</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>红</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>细麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>织</rb><rt>Tsih</rt><rb>花头</rb><rt>hwô-deo</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>以弗</rb><rt>Yi-feh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>织出个</rb><rt>tsih-cʽih-go</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>蓝</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>红</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>细麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>披肩</rb><rt>pʽi-kyin</rt><rb>连来</rb><rt>lin-læ</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt><rb>连拢</rb><rt>lin-long</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>以弗</rb><rt>Yi-feh</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>织出</rb><rt>tsih-cʽih</rt><rb>花纹个</rb><rt>hwô-veng-go</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>以弗个</rb><rt>yi-feh-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>蓝</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>红</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>细麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>两块</rb><rt>liang-kwʽe</rt><rb>碧玉</rb><rt>pih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>刻</rb><rt>kʽeh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>六个</rb><rt>loh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>玉里</rb><rt>nyüoh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>六个</rb><rt>loh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>玉里</rb><rt>nyüoh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>次序</rb><rt>tsʽ-jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>刻</rb><rt>Kʽeh</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>刻印</rb><rt>kʽeh-ing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>刻</rb><rt>kʽeh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两块</rb><rt>liang-kwʽe</rt><rb>玉里</rb><rt>nyüoh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>嵌</rb><rt>kʽæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>座子里</rb><rt>zo-ts-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两块</rb><rt>liang-kwʽe</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>以弗个</rb><rt>yi-feh-go</rt><rb>肩胛头</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>记认个</rb><rt>kyi-nying-go</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>肩胛头</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>记认</rb><rt>kyi-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>编</rb><rt>Pin</rt><rb>两根</rb><rt>liang-keng</rt><rb>赤金</rb><rt>tsʽih-kying</rt><rb>丝</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>缠拢</rb><rt>dzin-long</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>绳</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>勾住</rb><rt>keo-djü</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>织出</rb><rt>tsih-cʽih</rt><rb>审判个</rb><rt>sing-pʽun-go</rt><rb>胸牌</rb><rt>Hyüong-ba</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尺工</rb><rt>tsʽih-kong</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>以弗个</rb><rt>yi-feh-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>蓝</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>红</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>细麻布</rb><rt>si-mô-pu</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>转方个</rb><rt>cün-fông-go</rt><rb>两层</rb><rt>liang-zeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>一庹</rb><rt>ih-tʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阔</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>一庹</rb><rt>ih-tʽoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>嵌</rb><rt>kʽæn</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四埭</rb><rt>s-da</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>头一</rb><rt>deo-ih</rt><rb>埭</rb><rt>da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>玛瑙</rb><rt>mô-nao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>淡黄</rb><rt>dæn-wông</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>透光</rb><rt>tʽeo-kwông</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>埭</rb><rt>da</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>埭</rb><rt>da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葱玉</rb><rt>tsʽong-nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>青玉</rb><rt>tsʽing-nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>金刚石</rb><rt>kying-kông-zah</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>Di-sæn</rt><rb>埭</rb><rt>da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赤玉</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>玛瑙</rb><rt>mô-nao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫玉</rb><rt>ts-nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>第四</rb><rt>Di-s</rt><rb>埭</rb><rt>da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>绿玉</rb><rt>loh-nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>苍玉</rb><rt>tsʽông-nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>碧玉</rb><rt>pih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嵌来</rb><rt>kʽæn-læ</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>座子里</rb><rt>zo-ts-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>玉</rb><rt>Nyüoh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>块</rb><rt>kwʽe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>刻</rb><rt>kʽeh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>玉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>胸牌个</rb><rt>hyüong-ba-go</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>两根</rb><rt>liang-keng</rt><rb>赤金</rb><rt>tsʽih-kying</rt><rb>丝</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>缠拢</rb><rt>dzin-long</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>绳</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>胸牌</rb><rt>hyüong-ba</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>圈</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>圈</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>胸牌个</rb><rt>hyüong-ba-go</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两根</rb><rt>liang-keng</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>绳</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>挂来</rb><rt>kwô-læ</rt><rb>胸</rb><rt>hyüong</rt><rb>牌个</rb><rt>ba-go</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>圈里</rb><rt>kyʽün-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两根</rb><rt>liang-keng</rt><rb>链条个</rb><rt>lin-diao-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>座子</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>连带拢</rb><rt>lin-tæn-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安来</rb><rt>en-læ</rt><rb>以弗个</rb><rt>yi-feh-go</rt><rb>肩胛头</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>圈</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>胸牌</rb><rt>hyüong-ba</rt><rb>两边个</rb><rt>liang-pin-go</rt><rb>沿里</rb><rt>yin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>以弗个</rb><rt>yi-feh-go</rt><rb>里半边</rb><rt>li-pun-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>圈</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放来</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>以弗</rb><rt>yi-feh</rt><rb>两边个</rb><rt>liang-pin-go</rt><rb>下头</rb><rt>ʽô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>对着</rb><rt>te-djoh</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>连拢</rb><rt>lin-long</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>以弗个</rb><rt>yi-feh-go</rt><rb>织出</rb><rt>tsih-cʽih</rt><rb>花带</rb><rt>hwô-ta</rt><rb>子</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>胸牌个</rb><rt>Hyüong-ba-go</rt><rb>圈</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>蓝带</rb><rt>læn-ta</rt><rb>缚住</rb><rt>bo-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>以弗个</rb><rt>yi-feh-go</rt><rb>圈</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>连拢</rb><rt>lin-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>织出</rb><rt>tsih-cʽih</rt><rb>花带</rb><rt>hwô-ta</rt><rb>子</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>胸牌</rb><rt>hyüong-ba</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>以弗</rb><rt>yi-feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>圣所</rb><rt>Sing-sô</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>审判个</rb><rt>sing-pʽun-go</rt><rb>胸牌</rb><rt>hyüong-ba</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带来</rb><rt>ta-læ</rt><rb>胸脘头</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>记认</rb><rt>kyi-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>乌陵</rb><rt>U-ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>土明</rb><rt>Tʽu-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>放来</rb><rt>fông-læ</rt><rb>审判个</rb><rt>sing-pʽun-go</rt><rb>胸牌里</rb><rt>hyüong-ba-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>胸脘头</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>胸脘头</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>时刻</rb><rt>z-kʽeh</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>纯蓝</rb><rt>jing-læn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>以弗</rb><rt>yi-feh</rt><rb>里向个</rb><rt>li-hyiang-go</rt><rb>套衣</rb><rt>Tʽao-i</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>洞</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>洞</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>织出个</rb><rt>tsih-cʽih-go</rt><rb>花头</rb><rt>hwô-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>沿</rb><rt>yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>盔甲</rb><rt>kwʽe-kah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>洞</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>扯开</rb><rt>tsʽô-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>下沿</rb><rt>ʽô-yin</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>蓝</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>红</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>石榴</rb><rt>zih-liu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>沿里</rb><rt>yin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>响铃</rb><rt>hyiang-ling</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>夹拢的</rb><rt>kah-long-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>金铃</rb><rt>kying-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>石榴</rb><rt>zih-liu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>金铃</rb><rt>kying-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>石榴</rb><rt>zih-liu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>套衣</rb><rt>tʽao-i</rt><rb>团圈个</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün-go</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>沿</rb><rt>yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>穿仔</rb><rt>cʽün-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>办事</rb><rt>bæn-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>圣所</rb><rt>Sing-sô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走出个</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>丧命</rb><rt>sông-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>册子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt><rb>过个</rb><rt>ko-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>廿岁</rb><rt>nyiæn-shü</rt><rb>以</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>有佬个</rb><rt>Yiu-lao-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>穷个</rb><rt>gyüong-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>减落</rb><rt>kæn-loh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只要</rb><rt>tsih-iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>*z-kʽeh*</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>取赎个</rb><rt>cʽü-joh-go</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>帐篷个</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>用场</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孙个</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>记认</rb><rt>kyi-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>铜</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>浴盆</rb><rt>yüoh-beng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>铜</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>安来</rb><rt>en-læ</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>坛个</rb><rt>dæn-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>𣥼的</rb><rt>tsi-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>燔祭</rb><rt>væn-tsi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伦</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt><rb>永远个</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和华</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>燔祭个</rb><rt>væn-tsi-go</rt><rb>坛</rb><rt>dæn</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>浴盆</rb><rt>yüoh-beng</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup>
